**[00:02:53.460] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-000017)

**[00:02:59.860] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-000018)  
The Committee recognizes CHED's important gains during the current budget year.

**[00:03:04.740] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-000019)  
Under Fiscal Year 2026 General Appropriations Act,

**[00:03:08.740] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-000020)  
37.5 billion was allocated for universal access to quality tertiary education,

**[00:03:15.940] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-000021)

**[00:03:22.820] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-000022)  
Beneficiary

**[00:03:23.860] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-000023)

**[00:03:33.620] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-000024)

**[00:03:41.860] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-000025)

**[00:03:48.180] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-000026)

**[00:03:53.780] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-000027)

**[00:04:00.980] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-000028)

**[00:04:08.660] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-000029)

**[00:04:14.740] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-000030)

**[00:04:20.660] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-000031)  
Technology.

**[00:04:22.740] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-000032)

**[00:04:27.280] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-000033)  
The Commission and Land Bank entered into agreements intended to address long-standing

**[00:04:32.100] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-000034)  
delays in scholarship and grant releases and streamline the disbursement process.

**[00:04:37.980] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-000035)

**[00:04:43.920] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-000036)  
implementation, validation, billing, and liquidation procedures.

**[00:04:49.760] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-000037)

**[00:04:56.380] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-000038)

**[00:05:01.520] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-000039)

**[00:05:14.420] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-000041)  
and inequalities in access.

**[00:05:17.130] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-000042)

**[00:05:21.850] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-000043)  
With these objectives, the Committee looks forward to

**[00:05:24.910] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-000044)  
CHED's presentation of its Fiscal

**[00:05:26.790] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-000045)

**[00:05:32.530] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-000046)

Just a short introduction. I was reading Congressman Jude Acidre's article on education reform

**[00:06:24.340] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000057)  
this morning, and he talked about three things that we need to do if we are serious about

**[00:06:31.420] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000058)  
moving education forward.

**[00:06:33.100] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000061)  
3S: Sustain, Strengthen, and Synchronize.

**[00:06:38.710] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000062)

**[00:06:51.880] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000063)  
Because to sustain honorable congressmen, we need to continue supporting our students and existing commitments.

**[00:07:00.920] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000064)  
To strengthen, we need to invest in our institutions and systems.

**[00:07:06.980] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000065)

**[00:07:17.820] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000066)

**[00:07:24.440] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000067)

leaving nearly half of our identified priority unfunded project.

With the effort to ensure our community is centered growth for an inclusive, resilient future, the proposed fiscal year 2027 National Expenditure Program, formally submitted to Congress on August 11, amounts to $7.2 trillion.

**[00:08:47.640] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000078)  
The education sector is very much happy to be the highest budget priority, receiving $1 trillion for three education agencies.

**[00:08:58.140] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000079)

**[00:09:13.460] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000080)

**[00:49:25.340] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000436)

**[00:49:40.560] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000437)  
Our operation is based on the school year.

**[00:49:46.220] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000438)

**[00:49:53.240] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000439)  
So in fact,

**[00:49:55.200] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000440)  
kasi yung cut-off na pinakita po namin as of June 30, 2025, as of this date po, ay marami na pong naiprocess.

**[00:50:08.360] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000441)

**[00:50:17.180] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000442)  
we will have a so much increased obligation of our allotment.

**[00:50:23.520] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000443)  
Thank you for that clarification, Madam Chair.

**[00:50:27.700] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000444)  
Pumunta naman po tayo dun sa TES, Tertiary Education Subsidy. Although, well, we know from the data that nakakatulong talaga siya.

**[00:50:39.220] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000445)  
According to the EDCOM 2, dahil sa TES, bumaba ng 57.7% ang gastos ng mga pamilya pagdating sa college.

**[00:50:49.280] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000446)  
At tumaas naman from 4.6% to 19.7%

**[00:50:55.340] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000447)  
ang bahagi ng poorest

**[00:50:57.020] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000448)  
decile sa mga studyanteng naka-enroll sa mga SUC.

**[00:51:02.520] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000449)  
But dahil siguro din na nakakasiksikan na tayo sa mga SUC,

**[00:51:08.200] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000450)  
when we speak of the poorest, yung mga nasa 4Ps who are graduating from grade 12,

**[00:51:16.760] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000451)  
nasa 5.54% lang ang nakaka-enroll sa kolehyo gamit ang TES.

**[00:51:22.740] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000452)  
At this is very far from the 40% target

**[00:51:27.280] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000453)  
of the government.

**[00:51:28.540] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000454)  
Kaya ang unang tanong po dito ay, ano ba ang plano ng CHED para mas maraming 4P students ang matarget at makakuha ng TES?

**[00:51:38.120] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000455)  
Since talaga naman dapat sila ang, dito tayo nakatutok.

**[00:51:45.980] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000456)  
Thank you again for that question, sir.

**[00:51:48.340] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000457)  
Unang-una po ay let me mention that we are aware of that

**[00:51:56.580] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000458)  
Unang-una is yung take in 4Ps

**[00:51:59.860] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000459)  
So ang ginawa po namin is we launched the caravan, the information caravan

**[00:52:05.800] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000460)  
We call it PBBM Gabay, patuloy na edukasyon, patuloy ng pag-angat sa buhay

**[00:52:12.740] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000461)  
And then we partnered po with the DSWD

**[00:52:17.460] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000462)  
So hindi na po kami nag-validate.

**[00:52:20.060] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000463)  
So we have the number.

**[00:52:21.160] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000464)  
So kung intake yung pagpasok ng 4Ps natin ay hindi na po problema.

**[00:52:27.580] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000465)  
In fact, this 2026,

**[00:52:30.400] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000466)  
we requested based on the DSWD data, we requested about 50% of the 423,000 identified 4Ps.

**[00:52:41.980] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000467)  
Yun lang kasi nga based on past, mga 30% lang po ang takers.

**[00:52:48.180] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000468)  
But this year, we target 50% of the DSWD list.

**[00:52:53.300] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000469)  
To reach them out po, umikot po kami.

**[00:52:56.380] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000470)  
And now, it is overwhelming.

**[00:52:59.020] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000471)  
Kung alam pala ng mga 4Ps na meron silang buhay at future and hope,

**[00:53:05.820] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000472)  
Ngayon po, 1.2 million na po ang nag-apply at bina-validate namin.

**[00:53:12.680] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000473)  
Susunod na tanong po is bakit ang baba ng graduation rate?

**[00:53:18.200] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000474)  
Yan po ang masakit na katotohanan

**[00:53:20.340] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000475)  
because while we give free higher education to our 4Ps

**[00:53:25.560] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000476)  
at binibigyan natin sila ng different subsidies,

**[00:53:30.000] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000477)  
one is the tertiary education subsidy which is only 12,000 per sem,

**[00:53:34.800] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000478)  
We also have tulong dunong na palaging sinasabi ng ating butihing congressman

**[00:53:41.660] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000479)  
paano sila mabubuhay ng 7,500 per sem?

**[00:53:45.580] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000480)  
Aware po kami dyan, but we only operate by the mandate of the provision of the GAA.

**[00:53:52.440] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000481)  
Kaya pag-usapan po natin mamaya to address the attrition rate.

**[00:53:56.560] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000482)  
Aware din po kami na ang reason po,

**[00:53:59.660] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000483)  
ng mataas ng attrition rate is yung daily subsistence allowance nila.

**[00:54:05.320] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000484)  
And ang isang bottleneck nila is yung mataas na pagbayad ng kanilang dormitories or accommodation, sir.

**[00:54:16.400] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000485)  
Kaya po nakipag-partner kami ngayon with Pag-IBIG to

**[00:54:20.940] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000486)  
in order to empower the SUCs kasi po ang hirap.

**[00:54:25.240] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000487)  
Ngayon po almost zero lahat ang SUC ng infrastructure.

**[00:54:29.280] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000488)  
If we maintain that, walang maipapatayo na dormitory.

**[00:54:32.800] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000489)  
Kaya po nakipag-partner kami sa Pag-IBIG,

**[00:54:35.800] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000490)  
we signed already and to date there are already 100 plus SUCs

**[00:54:39.840] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000491)  
for them to avail of the pag -ibig loan pabahay

**[00:54:43.520] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000492)  
at a rate of 3% rather than 8% per annum.

**[00:54:48.800] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000493)  
Pero ngayon po rin na -prepare po namin

**[00:54:51.580] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000494)  
one dormitory to be built by the SUC to house these students which

**[00:54:58.180] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000495)  
is now ranging

**[00:54:59.020] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000496)  
from 10,000 and above to as high as 62,000 students per SUC.

**[00:55:05.260] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000497)  
Ay nag-propose kami to our House Speaker through Congressman Jude

**[00:55:10.260] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000498)  
na kung pwede ang gobyerno ay komplementaran

**[01:15:00.050] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001016)  
Sa 2027 budget po, ano ang component ng CHED para i-implement ang Labor Education Act? Kung meron po, kindly discuss it, Madam Chairperson.

**[01:15:20.980] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001017)  
Yeah, kasama po sa aming, I'm just scanning my notes, kasama po siya sa MNE Higher Education Program na hindi binigay is 258 million. So, this may be one na masasagasaan kung hindi po ibibigay ito.

**[01:15:42.310] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001018)  
Thank you, Madam Chairperson, for pointing that out. So, kailangan po natin na pondohan ang implementation ng Labor Education Act.

**[01:15:50.640] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001019)  
Sa roll-out plan po for academic year 2026-2027, ang labor education po ay dinideliver as a 12-hour component embedded in NSTP or GE courses. Meron po bang plano ang CHED na i-expand ang labor education as a stand-alone credit-bearing subject?

**[01:16:11.940] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001020)  
Yes po. Immediately after we jointly signed with DOLE, ay meron na kaming two implementation big-ticket tracks. One is curriculum integration.

**[01:16:22.980] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001021)  
And the other one is the institutional implementation. So, may mga degree program po kasi na nag-consult kami na baka hindi pwedeng i-integrate sa curriculum, so ginag-incorporate sa NSTP.

**[01:16:43.100] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001022)  
Thank you, Madam Chairperson. Now, moving on, on TFI policy, RA 6728, Section 5.

**[01:16:52.160] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001023)  
Ano po 'yun, RA?

**[01:16:55.220] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001024)  
RA 6728, Section 5. So, that's the GASP law. 70% ng tuition fees ay dapat pumapunta sa mga manggagawa sa mga educational institutions. Aware po ba kayo dito, Madam Chairperson?

**[01:17:11.100] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001025)  
Yes po. We develop a good equation so that it will be inclusive and equitable across region. We have the data, Honorable.

**[01:17:18.720] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001026)  
Ngayon po ang problema dito, Madam Chairperson, in practice, hindi po ito ginagawa ng mga private HEIs. What is very explicit in the law, hindi po pinapatupad ng mga private HEIs.

**[01:17:32.440] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001027)  
So whenever there is a tuition fee increase, hindi nakukuha ng mga manggagawa sa mga private HEIs yung 70% share nila. Would you care to comment on this, Madam Chairperson?

**[01:17:58.380] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001028)  
Can I ask a friend?

**[01:17:59.900] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001029)  
Yes po, Madam Chairperson.

**[01:18:00.940] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001030)  
May I? Kasi best na ibubulong niya sa akin, sasabihin nilang, but as an introduction po, I met already the heads and officials of the higher education institution in the office regarding the tuition fee increase.

**[01:18:17.440] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001031)  
In fact, before the crisis, the oil crisis in the Middle East, pumunta po sila sa amin and I thank them because they listened to us.

**[01:18:28.980] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001032)  
Let us comply with the EO, the President, and the memorandum order to hold the tuition fee increase.

**[01:18:37.280] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001033)  
But before we give the permission for the increase, we have criteria to be met.

**[01:18:44.720] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001034)  
Yes po, Madam Chair. Ang tanong ko po is, hindi po doon patungkol kung meron bang tuition fee increase o wala. Ang tanong ko po is, bakit hindi nakukuha ng mga teachers, staff, yung 70% share nila sa tuition fee increase?

**[01:19:02.395] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001035)  
Okay.

**[01:19:02.615] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001036)  
And whenever these staff will go to CHED, ang sinasabi po palagi ng CHED, refer to your respective management for appropriate action. So I would like to get the clarification of CHED regarding this.

**[01:19:16.120] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001037)  
Part of the signed documents by the private higher education when we give the final clearance for tuition fee is a certification to comply with the provision of RA 6728, Section 5.

**[01:19:34.480] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001038)  
Nagsa-sign po naman sila.

**[01:19:38.080] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001039)  
Unfortunately, I do not know, correct me if I'm wrong, that we have not received any complaint from the faculty in charge. Have we received? Or even, we have the e-Tugon, Congressman. We have already strengthened our online e-Tugon system. You please go to CHED website.

**[01:20:00.080] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001040)  
Madam Chairperson, ang Council of Teachers and Staffs of Colleges and Universities of the Philippines, COTESCO.

**[01:20:06.280] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001041)  
This is the biggest alliance. Lagi pong pumupunta sa CHED. Specifically, CEU-FAO.

**[01:20:25.580] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001042)  
Ito, Madam Chair, there is a communication from CHED na sinasabi po dito na refer na lang to the management regarding this matter. And this is happening elsewhere po sa mga private HEIs natin.

**[01:20:44.340] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001043)  
Yeah, we will. Ikaw na lang. We will address this.

**[01:20:48.880] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001044)  
We will address this. And I am told that Director Corr is the Director of the Student Services. May I be permitted to give the answer more detailed, Honorable Congressman?

**[01:21:08.140] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001045)  
Yes, please introduce yourself lang. Kahit doon ka na lang before you proceed to answer. Thank you.

**[01:21:16.080] CHED Director Corinna Frances Cabanilla** (asr-001046)  
Good afternoon po. I am Cor Cabanilla, the Director of Office of Student Development and Services.

**[01:21:22.300] CHED Director Corinna Frances Cabanilla** (asr-001047)  
Regarding your concern, we are already talking with COTESCO on the matter. We received their concern and we immediately have a scheduled meeting with them.

**[01:21:34.640] CHED Director Corinna Frances Cabanilla** (asr-001048)  
We understand as well that it is a labor concern. So to facilitate the matter, we will just help them coordinate with the Department of Labor and Employment

**[01:21:46.560] CHED Director Corinna Frances Cabanilla** (asr-001049)  
so that we also do not cross any jurisdiction pertaining to the matter.

**[01:21:50.120] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001051)  
Yes, we understand that it is a labor issue, but it is also within your mandate.

**[01:21:56.360] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001052)  
Section 9.2, 9.3, 9.4 of Memorandum Order No. 3, Series of 2012, duty po ng CHED.

**[01:22:05.700] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001053)  
Okay, ako na. Thank you very much for reminding us this, Congressman. We will look into this immediately.

**[01:22:14.600] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001054)  
Thank you, Madam Chairperson.

**[01:22:22.340] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001057)  
We will, because we already signed memorandum.

**[01:22:26.420] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001058)  
Thank you, Madam Chairperson. My next question would be on the MORPHE.

**[01:22:31.400] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001059)  
Sabi niyo po, last year, 360 days na nakalipas. 100 days, on your first 100 days ay a-address niyo po itong MORPHE. Ano na po ang status dito, Madam Chairperson?

**[01:22:50.920] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001060)  
We already have the draft.

**[01:23:00.220] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001062)  
Yeah, the promise is not tabled. It's not tabled. We immediately form a technical working group across SUC to review all our policies.

**[01:23:11.860] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001063)  
As I mentioned earlier, there are 1,000-plus policies which have never been reviewed in the past.

**[01:23:18.080] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001064)  
But because of the limited number of personnel in CHED, we tap the best lawyers across SUCs. And one of this is MORPHE already.

**[01:23:27.260] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001065)  
So we already have a comprehensive revision of the Manual of Regulation of Private Higher Education,

**[01:23:33.580] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001066)  
updating the primary regulatory instrument governing private higher education institutions to reflect.

**[01:23:40.560] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001067)  
And as of last meeting in our CEB, we will have the last scheduled consultation, Honorable Congressman, on September 15 to 17.

**[01:23:53.240] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001068)  
We will present our draft and after such getting input of the private higher education, we will be able to publish them.

**[01:24:01.820] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001072)  
Maraming salamat po, Madam Chairperson. Maganda po na nabanggit niyo na meron na pong tentative schedule.

**[01:24:07.160] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001073)  
Dahil po mula October 23, 2025, that's the preliminary meeting for MORPHE review and the second meeting was done on November 19, 2025.

**[01:24:16.680] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001074)  
Hindi pa po nakakuha ng update yung COTESCO, but it is good na sinabi niyo po na meron nang tentative schedule.

**[01:24:23.320] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001075)  
Madam Chairperson, alam niyo po kung bakit ko binibigyan din ito?

**[01:24:26.860] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001076)  
Dahil yung MORPHE is in direct violation of the Labor Code.

**[01:24:31.760] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001077)  
Inadmit niyo rin po ito ng CHED during the last budget hearing.

**[01:24:35.980] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001078)  
Malinaw sa Labor Code, ang probationary period is six months only.

**[01:24:40.960] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001079)  
Pero dito sa MORPHE, six consecutive semesters.

**[01:24:46.340] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001080)  
Ibig sabihin, ang probationary period for workers in private HEIs ay tatlong taon.

**[01:24:53.740] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001081)  
So malinaw na sinusupersede ng MORPHE yung nakasulat sa ating Labor Code.

**[01:25:01.120] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001082)  
Kaya maganda po na ito ay mapag-usapan na natin dahil ginagamit po ito, Madam Chairperson,

**[01:25:08.500] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001083)  
ng mga private higher educational institutions para perpetually hindi ma-regular yung kanilang mga faculty.

**[01:25:16.800] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001084)  
At ginagamit din po nila ito bilang paraan para arbitrarily tanggalin na lang sila

**[01:25:23.340] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001085)  
o i-deload o hindi na pagturuin doon sa mga private HEIs.

**[01:25:27.340] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001086)  
Makukuha po ba namin ng commitment ng Commission on Higher Education regarding this, Madam Chairperson?

**[01:25:32.360] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001087)  
I will personally monitor the final draft, Honorable Congressman, after September 15 to 17.

**[01:25:40.640] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001088)  
And we will update you gladly.

**[01:25:42.280] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001089)  
Maraming salamat po, Madam Chairperson.

**[01:25:44.440] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001090)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[01:25:48.140] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-001092)

**[01:25:54.640] Rep. Jude A. Acidre** (asr-001094)

**[01:26:02.740] Rep. Jude A. Acidre** (asr-001095)

**[01:26:09.220] Rep. Jude A. Acidre** (asr-001096)  
At nagpapasalamat po tayo sa CHED na bagamat pansin naman po natin yung kakulangan sa resources at sa budget,

**[01:26:20.680] Rep. Jude A. Acidre** (asr-001097)

**[01:26:23.160] Rep. Jude A. Acidre** (asr-001098)

**[01:26:28.600] Rep. Jude A. Acidre** (asr-001099)  
Madam Chair, base po sa datos na meron po na-present po ninyo,

**[01:26:34.600] Rep. Jude A. Acidre** (asr-001100)

**[01:26:43.200] Rep. Jude A. Acidre** (asr-001101)

**[01:26:50.300] Rep. Jude A. Acidre** (asr-001102)

**[01:26:56.800] Rep. Jude A. Acidre** (asr-001103)  
Nangangahulugan po na 137,000 students ang hindi po makaka-avail ng free higher education

**[01:27:04.780] Rep. Jude A. Acidre** (asr-001104)  
at around 833,000 students naman ang mawawala sa TES.

**[01:27:14.020] Rep. Jude A. Acidre** (asr-001105)

**[01:27:20.880] Rep. Jude A. Acidre** (asr-001106)  
kasi mukha pong magkakaroon po tayo dahil sa funding cap ng free higher education,

**[01:27:29.720] Rep. Jude A. Acidre** (asr-001107)  
eh mukhang mapipilitan po ang ating mga SUC na tumanggap lamang ng mga estudyante na pasok po sa pondong nakalaan sa kanila.

**[01:27:38.900] Rep. Jude A. Acidre** (asr-001108)  
At naaalala po ako kasi if this becomes a pattern, ang lalabas po nito magkakaroon po ng progressive na decrease

**[01:27:49.040] Rep. Jude A. Acidre** (asr-001109)

**[01:27:53.900] Rep. Jude A. Acidre** (asr-001110)

**[01:27:57.900] Rep. Jude A. Acidre** (asr-001111)

**[01:28:10.060] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001112)  
Madam Chair.

**[01:28:13.060] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001113)  
Yeah, napakabigat po ang tanong na yan kong,

**[01:28:16.920] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001114)  
but as our...

**[01:28:18.700] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001115)  
our mandate now, we always go back to the data.

**[01:28:23.720] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001116)  
So, nag-umpisa kaming nag-collect ng data, ng initial simulation, no, ng 541 SUC.

**[01:28:31.260] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001117)  
Ito yung data po, bago ko sagutin yun.

**[01:28:34.560] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001118)  
There are, of the 541 state, university, and colleges, including their campuses,

**[01:28:41.660] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001119)  
68% po exceeded their estimated enrollment capacity.

**[01:28:47.260] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001120)  
Bakit kaya?

**[01:28:48.440] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001121)

**[01:28:53.880] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001122)  
Yung 32% within or below their estimated enrollment capacity po.

**[01:29:00.140] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001123)  
Now, nag-agree na po kami ng student-faculty ratio.

**[01:29:04.520] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001124)  
Kung iano namin yung faculty insufficient, so,

**[01:29:08.180] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001125)  
ang rason po ng aming...

**[01:29:12.400] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001126)  
because of this data, we already sit down, in fact, with the...

**[01:29:18.780] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001127)  
Department of Budget, to come up with an equation,

**[01:29:23.560] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001128)  
basing on the 1 is to 40 faculty-student ratio.

**[01:29:29.540] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001129)  
So, meron na po kaming number, and we are trying to implement this.

**[01:29:35.540] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001130)  
Ito lang yung capacity ninyo, ito lang muna ang kukunin ninyo.

**[01:29:39.320] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001131)  
But through the years, based on the data, kahit sinabi na noon na you only enroll yung kaya nyo,

**[01:29:47.500] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001132)  
they keep admitting.

**[01:29:49.200] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001134)  
So, ang recommendation na number one po,

**[01:29:52.200] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001135)  
in as much as we are increasing the classroom of our...

**[01:29:58.060] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001137)  
basic education, we really need

**[01:30:02.140] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001138)  
to give attention to the increase

**[01:30:04.480] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001139)  
in the classroom of our

**[01:30:06.620] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001140)  
higher education. Kasi po

**[01:30:08.440] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001141)

**[01:30:10.540] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001142)

**[01:30:12.380] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001143)  
pero hindi ginalaw ang infrastructure

**[01:30:14.680] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001144)  
ng higher ed, saan po

**[01:30:16.080] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001145)  
namin sila ikukulong?

**[01:30:18.320] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001146)  
That's number one question po. Number two

**[01:30:20.260] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001147)  
po, is the faculty.

**[01:30:23.100] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001148)  
Faculty items.

**[01:30:25.000] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001149)  
So, kung

**[01:30:26.360] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001150)  
ang existing enrollment nila

**[01:30:27.960] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001151)  
is beyond the 1 is to

**[01:30:29.880] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001152)  
40 faculty-student

**[01:30:31.860] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001153)  
ratio, ang sasagot

**[01:30:34.180] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001154)  
naman sa increased item

**[01:30:36.080] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001155)  
ng faculty is

**[01:30:37.480] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001156)  
DBM. So,

**[01:30:40.360] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001157)  
itong equation na ito

**[01:30:42.020] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001158)  
is very complex.

**[01:30:44.500] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001159)  
So, kung kailangan natin ipagpatuloy

**[01:30:46.700] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001160)  
ang continuous increase

**[01:30:48.600] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001161)  
on the admission of our

**[01:30:50.060] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001162)  
Filipino learners who are

**[01:30:51.920] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001163)  
qualified to higher education,

**[01:30:54.640] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001164)  
there should be a corresponding

**[01:30:56.140] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001165)  
increase in the infrastructure

**[01:30:58.660] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001166)

**[01:31:00.120] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001167)  
in faculty, and

**[01:31:01.640] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001168)  
corresponding increase

**[01:31:04.000] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001169)  
in the non-teaching position.

**[01:31:06.380] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001170)  
Because there is also

**[01:31:08.120] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001171)  
a ratio. On top of that,

**[01:31:10.960] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001172)  
much as we would like

**[01:31:12.180] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001173)  
to absorb more Filipino

**[01:31:13.980] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001174)  
learners, sir,

**[01:31:15.680] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001175)  
we do not want to absorb

**[01:31:17.920] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001176)  
them just to have a diploma.

**[01:31:20.600] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001177)  
We want to graduate

**[01:31:22.120] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001178)  
Filipino learners

**[01:31:23.200] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001179)  
who are industry-ready

**[01:31:25.640] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001180)  
and competitive. With that,

**[01:31:27.820] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001181)  
papasok yung isang factor.

**[01:31:30.740] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001182)  
That is the enhancement

**[01:31:31.700] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001183)  
of funding for the facilities.

Maraming salamat, Madam Chair,

**[01:31:36.780] Rep. Jude A. Acidre** (asr-001185)

**[01:32:08.940] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001199)  
Thank you again for that question,

**[01:32:11.180] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001200)  
sir.

**[01:32:13.860] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001201)  
This is very

**[01:32:14.740] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001202)

**[01:32:17.560] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001203)  
Across 115

**[01:32:18.500] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001204)  
SUCs,

**[01:32:20.500] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001205)  
I'll give the number.

**[01:32:27.180] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001206)  
there are five SUC

**[01:32:28.300] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001207)  
who collects tuition fee

**[01:32:30.540] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001208)  
at 50 pesos

**[01:32:32.740] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001209)  
per unit

**[01:32:35.460] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001210)  
Until now.

**[01:32:36.800] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001212)  
There are 32 state universities

**[01:32:40.720] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001213)  
who collect tuition fee

**[01:32:42.420] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001214)  
per unit from 51 to 100.

**[01:32:47.040] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001215)  
There are 57 SUC

**[01:32:49.800] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001216)  
who collect 101 to 200

**[01:32:52.680] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001217)  
per unit.

**[01:32:54.920] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001218)  
Only 14

**[01:32:57.120] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001219)  
are collecting

**[01:32:58.700] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001220)  
200 to 300

**[01:33:00.060] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001221)  
per unit.

**[01:33:02.400] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001222)  
And five collects

**[01:33:03.760] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001223)  
400 pesos to

**[01:33:06.320] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001224)  
1,000 per unit.

**[01:33:07.940] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001225)  
That is the discrepancy.

**[01:33:09.840] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001227)  
across state, university, and colleges.

**[01:33:13.480] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001228)  
Kaya po,

**[01:33:14.400] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001229)  
ang provision

**[01:33:17.860] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001230)  
of the UniFAST should be

**[01:33:20.000] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001231)  
to come up with a composite fee

**[01:33:21.600] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001232)  
five years after the

**[01:33:23.840] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001233)

Panglimang taon na ba ngayon?

**[01:33:29.280] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001235)

ay wala pang

**[01:33:33.520] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001237)

**[01:33:35.140] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001238)

**[01:33:37.220] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001239)

**[01:33:39.080] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001240)

**[01:33:41.120] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001241)  
an SGV

**[01:33:42.400] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001242)  
and then to study

**[01:33:44.640] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001243)  
a scientific

**[01:33:46.060] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001244)  
analysis

**[01:33:49.780] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001245)  
of the data

**[01:33:50.320] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001246)  
that contributes

**[01:33:52.520] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001247)  
to a common

**[01:33:55.060] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001248)  
equation.

**[01:33:55.720] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001249)  
para po regardless of the location

**[01:33:59.200] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001250)  
of the state university and

**[01:34:01.140] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001251)  
colleges, parehas po.

**[01:34:02.840] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001252)  
So we have already the data.

**[01:34:05.240] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001253)  
We presented

**[01:34:06.460] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001254)  
so ang SGV,

**[01:34:09.000] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001255)  
nagbigay po ng

**[01:34:10.080] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001256)  
comparative funding computation,

**[01:43:00.030] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001505)

**[01:43:56.210] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001533)

**[01:45:47.420] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001573)  
My next question is, in 2025, Tulong Dunong had 2

**[01:45:53.480] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001574)  
.057 billion, but zero obligation and zero disbursement.

**[01:46:01.810] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001575)

**[01:46:06.450] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001576)

**[01:46:13.330] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001577)

**[01:46:20.710] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001578)

**[01:46:26.810] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001579)

**[01:46:33.890] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001579-split-1)  
Every scholarship program being

**[01:46:37.150] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001580)

**[01:46:42.150] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001581)

**[01:46:48.430] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001582)  
education subsidy noong 2025 po, unfortunately,

**[01:46:53.350] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001583)  
yung

**[01:46:54.610] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001584)  
system is not as systematic. Kaya po nag-sign kami ng DSWD

**[01:47:00.310] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001585)  
na sabi ko, it is not the job of CHED to validate on the ground. You submit to us

**[01:47:06.610] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001586)  
the clean data of DSWD. So, nag-umpisa po itong 2026, we have already the clean

**[01:47:13.530] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001587)  
list. So, yung 2025, although hindi po namin iniwan ang 2025, kaya po nag-catch

**[01:47:21.370] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001588)  
up po kami. Kaya maraming masaya ngayon na bata

**[01:47:24.150] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001589)  
kasi nahabol po namin at binigyan. Sa Tulong Dunong po. So, yung 2025

**[01:47:32.630] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001590)  
na ki-natch up namin, Honorable Congressman, nung dumating po ako, 31% almost ang

**[01:47:39.290] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001591)  
disbursement. To date, we already have disbursed 99.84%.

**[01:47:46.290] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001592)  
Sa TDP po, ay ang nadatnan po. Come here. Sa TDP po, ang one reason kung

**[01:47:54.610] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001593)  
bakit

**[01:47:55.370] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001594)  
madaming hindi...  ng TDP is that the list is coming to our benefactors. The list po.

**[01:48:08.630] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001595)

**[01:48:12.950] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001596)  
Bine-verify po namin. We already have the approved guidelines. So, meron na kaming nakolekta for 2025 and we have already requested SARO.

**[01:48:26.530] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001597)  
Ang problema ko kasi ma'am, I understand kayo po yung bago nung last budget hearing.

**[01:48:35.390] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001598)

**[01:48:41.750] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001599)

**[01:48:47.490] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001600)  
2020, Tulong Dunong, 2 billion, zero obligation and zero disbursement.

**[01:48:56.110] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001601)

**[01:48:58.690] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001602)

400 beneficiaries.

**[01:49:18.830] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001605)

**[01:49:24.350] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001606)

**[01:49:26.570] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001607)  
ano nangyari,

**[01:49:27.990] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001608)

**[01:49:29.710] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001609)

**[01:49:40.670] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001610)

**[01:49:51.890] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001612)

**[01:49:53.730] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001613)

**[01:49:56.430] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001614)

**[01:49:59.850] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001615)

**[01:50:09.210] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001616)

**[01:50:23.450] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001619)

**[01:50:36.990] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001620)

**[01:50:44.830] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001621)

**[01:50:51.970] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001622)  
This year,

**[01:50:52.850] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001623)  
with deadline August 27 po.

**[01:50:56.290] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001624)

**[01:51:02.650] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-001625)

total to about

Now this year

ang loves to violate

**[02:07:04.150] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001747)

**[02:07:06.730] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001748)  
RA number 7722

**[02:07:11.570] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001750)  
Absent a

**[02:07:12.410] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001751)

**[02:07:13.210] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001752)  
retaining the HEDF

**[02:07:14.610] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001753)  
under the Bureau of Treasury

**[02:07:17.530] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001754)

**[02:07:19.330] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001755)  
Portfolio Manager

**[02:07:20.250] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001756)

**[02:07:22.030] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001757)

**[02:07:22.830] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001758)

**[02:07:24.690] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001759)

**[02:07:25.950] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001760)  
If the Bureau of Treasury

**[02:07:27.650] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001761)  
wants to hold

**[02:07:28.690] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001762)

**[02:07:29.950] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001763)

**[02:07:32.070] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001764)

**[02:07:35.640] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001766)

**[02:07:36.300] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001767)  
the Bureau of Treasury

**[02:07:38.570] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001768)

**[02:07:39.710] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001769)

**[02:07:41.370] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001775)

**[02:07:45.010] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001777)

**[02:07:46.950] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001778)

**[02:07:48.490] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001779)

**[02:07:49.930] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001780)

Hindi pwedeng executive order

yung ginamit po nila

**[02:07:55.930] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001783)

**[02:07:59.390] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001785)

**[02:08:00.130] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001786)  
If the law needs to be changed,

**[02:08:03.070] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001788)

**[02:08:04.070] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001789)

**[02:08:08.470] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001792)

**[02:08:31.270] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001810)  
CHED

**[03:00:37.520] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003246)

**[03:00:45.120] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003248)

**[03:00:59.820] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003252)

**[03:01:01.780] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003253)

**[03:01:06.680] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003254)  
Ang ginagawa namin ngayon sa commission, through our commissioners,

**[03:01:11.320] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003255)

**[03:01:19.540] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003256)

**[03:01:24.880] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003257)

**[03:01:28.840] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003258)  
But again,

**[03:01:29.660] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003259)

**[03:01:34.020] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003260)

**[03:01:35.300] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003261)

**[03:01:44.780] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003262)

**[03:01:49.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003263)

**[03:01:52.220] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003264)

**[03:01:54.060] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003266)  
Ginagawa po namin, minimum qualification lang po yun.

**[03:01:57.620] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003267)  
Minimum qualification po lang yan.

**[03:01:59.660] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003268)  
So the point, therefore, is yung instructor one should not be salary grade 12

**[03:02:05.820] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003269)  
since the minimum requirement is MA.

**[03:02:09.820] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003270)

**[03:02:20.530] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003274)

**[03:02:24.580] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003275)

sa mga instructors natin sa SUCs.

**[03:03:02.100] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003288)  
MA sila.

**[03:03:03.260] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003289)  
Pinaghihirapan yan bago kang makapag-MA.

**[03:03:06.480] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003290)  
Tapos ang entry-level position, salary grade 12.

**[03:03:10.640] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003291)  
Sa ibang mga ahensya, minimum 16.

**[03:03:14.400] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003292)  
Minimum 16.

**[03:03:15.940] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003293)  
Okay.

**[03:03:16.600] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003294)

**[03:04:04.210] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-003316)  
And good afternoon to the CHED family headed by Chairperson Agrupis.

**[03:04:12.280] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-003317)  
My first question is, is there a concept of an open university in the higher education?

**[03:04:24.200] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003318)  
Sorry po.

**[03:04:25.600] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003319)  
Ano po yun, Kong?

**[03:04:27.020] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-003320)  
Is there a concept of an open university in the higher education?

**[03:04:32.380] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003321)  
Yes po.

**[03:04:33.120] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003322)  
There is an open university.

**[03:04:36.800] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003323)  
We have guidelines.

**[03:04:38.120] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003324)  
We have guidelines for that.

**[03:04:39.100] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-003325)  
Can you give me a short, a brief summary of that concept?

**[03:04:46.830] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003326)  
Yeah.

**[03:04:48.850] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003327)  
The open university system is, the open university system is started,

**[03:04:56.950] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003328)  
the pioneering university on this is the University of the Philippines.

**[03:05:01.170] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003329)  
This will give opportunity to our learners to enroll at a distance.

**[03:05:06.870] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003330)  
At a distance po.

**[03:05:09.910] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003331)  
The curriculum that they are giving are packaged in a module.

**[03:05:15.350] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003332)  
In a package, in a module.

**[03:05:16.910] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003333)  
That's why part of the guidelines in evaluating,

**[03:05:20.690] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003334)  
part of the criteria in evaluating the application of SUC for open learning

**[03:05:26.770] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003335)  
is their internet connectivity, their e-learning system.

**[03:05:34.190] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003336)  
So kahit po may module sila,

**[03:05:36.250] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003337)  
if their internet connectivity and digital system is not allowing them,

**[03:05:42.050] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003338)  
hindi po namin inaaprobahan.

**[03:05:44.690] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-003339)  
Do you have a list of open universities in the Philippines?

**[03:05:49.990] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003340)  
Yes po, we have.

**[03:05:51.790] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003341)  
Can you have?

**[03:05:53.470] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003342)  
Silvet?

**[03:05:54.230] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003343)  
Okay.

**[03:05:54.950] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-003344)  
My next question.

**[03:05:56.650] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003345)  
We have here po, there are already about 31.

**[03:06:00.230] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003346)  
Out of 113, we have 31 universities who are already,

**[03:06:06.250] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003347)  
accredited or authorized to open a open university system

**[03:06:11.630] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003348)  
to a limited degree program only.

**[03:06:15.430] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-003349)  
My next question, Madam Terry.

**[03:06:19.050] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-003350)  
Is the Ipugo State University one of those authorized or allowed to have an open university?

**[03:06:28.130] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003351)  
I am checking now, Honorable Congressman,

**[03:06:30.890] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003352)  
but I see St. Louis University, Benguet State University,

**[03:06:38.410] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003354)  
No, unfortunately, wala pong Ifugao State University.

**[03:06:43.020] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-003355)  
Thank you.

**[03:06:43.880] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-003356)  
Kasi, my attention was to all,

**[03:06:48.160] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-003357)  
because there were questions when in the graduation,

**[03:06:52.020] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-003358)  
if you granted,

**[03:06:54.360] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-003359)  
to allow myself to graduate people from Danau

**[03:06:57.480] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-003360)  
who never attended physically in Ipugo State University.

**[03:07:03.540] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-003361)  
So I asked, bakit?

**[03:07:04.780] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-003362)  
Kasi maraming nagka-complain.

**[03:07:07.040] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-003363)  
Kasi, bakit?

**[03:07:07.560] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-003364)  
I want to open a university concept.

**[03:07:12.280] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-003365)  
Thank you for bringing that to our attention.

**[03:07:14.800] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-003366)  
We will definitely address that early morning on Monday.

**[03:07:18.220] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-003367)  
I brought that out, Madam Chair,

**[03:07:21.000] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-003368)  
because, alam mo, in the 14th Congress,

**[03:07:24.980] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-003369)  
when we were able to get the Ipugo State University,

**[03:07:30.080] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-003370)  
maraming atlaki sa akin

**[03:07:31.620] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-003371)  
na I was creating the Edipong Mill in the Cordillera.

**[03:07:36.940] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-003372)  
Because in the Cordillera,

**[03:07:38.620] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-003373)

**[03:07:41.480] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-003374)

**[03:07:43.160] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-003375)

**[03:07:44.760] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-003376)

**[03:07:46.880] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-003377)

**[03:07:51.380] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-003378)  
So,

**[03:07:52.500] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-003379)  
unfortunately,

**[03:07:55.340] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-003380)  
OIC,

**[03:07:56.040] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-003381)  
the director of the car state,

**[03:07:58.860] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-003382)

**[03:14:56.180] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-003612)  
good afternoon.

**[03:14:58.380] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-003613)  
Republic Act 10

**[03:14:59.820] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-003614)  
687, the UNIFAST Act, requires equity in the distribution of student assistance.

**[03:15:11.820] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-003615)  
It defines rationalized access as including increased participation of economically disadvantaged and marginalized sectors and equity in the distribution of economic resources.

**[03:15:27.940] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-003616)  
Paano niyo po— and this is your mandate, it's your constitutional mandate—

**[03:15:35.340] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-003617)  
paano niyo po dini-distribute yung inyong Tulong-Dunong Program?

**[03:15:54.460] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003618)

**[03:16:05.200] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-003619)  
In compliance with the essence of the law.

**[03:16:10.060] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-003620)  
Alright, let me just—

**[03:16:12.560] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-003621)  
The Tulong-Dunong Program is usually supported by an allocation put in by the representative themselves.

**[03:16:24.040] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-003622)  
Well—

**[03:16:24.900] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-003623)  
In this process, parang ano po ito? Pork barrel, ganoon?

**[03:16:30.760] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-003624)  
This will be unconstitutional.

**[03:16:34.940] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-003625)

**[03:16:44.060] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-003626)  
Kasi po, maliwanag dito eh.

**[03:16:45.860] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-003627)

**[03:16:53.040] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-003628)  
Chair declares suspension.

**[03:17:02.120] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-003628-split-1)

**[03:25:29.910] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003682)

**[03:25:39.170] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003685)

**[03:25:46.430] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003686)

**[03:25:50.070] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003687)

**[03:26:06.950] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-003688)  
Yan po yung nakakabahala.

**[03:26:09.070] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-003689)

**[03:26:17.830] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-003690)

**[03:26:21.850] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-003691)

**[03:26:34.050] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-003693)

**[03:26:40.070] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-003694)

**[03:26:46.630] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-003696)

**[03:26:54.010] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-003697)

**[03:27:01.270] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-003698)

**[03:27:10.510] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-003699)

**[03:27:18.530] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-003700)

**[03:27:36.150] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-003705)

**[03:27:39.670] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-003707)

**[03:27:43.750] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-003708)

**[03:27:54.990] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003712)  
Maraming salamat.

Kung mas popondohan yung TDP ngayon,

we will make 93,000 beneficiaries ngayong taon na ito very happy next year.

**[03:28:22.560] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-003715)  
Alright, we thank you the good representative.

**[03:28:25.880] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-003716)  
And now, from the minority, we recognize the

**[03:28:30.020] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-003717)  
Honorable Representative Sarah Elago.

**[03:28:33.960] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003718)  
Maraming salamat, Madam Chair.

**[03:28:36.360] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003719)  
Unang-una nais natin humingi ng ulat tungkol sa monitoring, evaluation,

**[03:28:43.320] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003720)  
implementation ng mandato ng CHED pagdating sa Bawal Bastos Law.

**[03:28:50.480] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003721)  
Section 25, Madam Chair, sinasabi dito na obligasyon ng CHED na magsagawa ng routine inspection

**[03:28:58.920] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003722)  
sa ating mga higher education institutions para matiyak

**[03:29:03.740] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003723)  
na sumusunod sa kung ano ang kinakailangan ng batas

**[03:29:07.080] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003724)  
para matiyak ang espasyo na malaya sa anumang forma ng gender-based sexual harassment.

**[03:29:15.440] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003725)  
Yes po, Ma'am.

**[03:29:17.900] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003726)  
Ini-implement po namin lahat every single provision ng safety spaces sa law,

**[03:29:24.880] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003727)  
Honorable Congresswoman.

**[03:29:26.280] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003728)  
In fact, every board meeting namin,

**[03:29:30.740] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003729)  
we either suspension or dismissal

**[03:29:34.680] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003730)  
na

**[03:29:35.220] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003731)  
napra-process na complaint sa amin when it comes to safety spaces.

**[03:29:41.660] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003732)  
Madam Chair, magkano ang nagastos para dito sa routine inspection?

**[03:29:46.320] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003733)  
Ilang HEIs po ang ating nabisita mula dun sa mga libo-libo nating institutions?

**[03:29:52.360] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003734)  
Nagagawa ba ito sa pampribado at pampubliko na mga institution?

**[03:29:56.900] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003735)  
At ano pa yung mga kinakailangan natin?

**[03:30:00.160] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003736)  
para sa susunod na taon para matiyak na meron tayong sapat na inspectors at matitiyak na magagawa din ng mga HEIs yung kanilang mga responsibilidad sa ilalim ng batas?

**[03:30:16.420] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003737)  
Yes po. We visit and we have a gender focal person in CHED to implement our prevention program.

**[03:30:27.560] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003738)  
And if you may allow, Honorable Chair, Vice Chair, may I request the focal person?

**[03:30:34.540] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003741)  
Director Legazpi, to please mention for the interest para masagot po nang gusto yung tanong.

**[03:30:53.600] CHED Regional Director IV Edna Imelda F. Legazpi** (asr-003742)  
Good afternoon, Madam Chair and the esteemed members.

**[03:30:57.580] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-003743)  
Yes, please introduce yourself, ma'am, before you proceed.

**[03:31:00.380] CHED Regional Director IV Edna Imelda F. Legazpi** (asr-003744)  
I'm Director Edna Imelda Legazpi of the RID of MIMAROPA.

**[03:31:06.080] CHED Regional Director IV Edna Imelda F. Legazpi** (asr-003746)  
And at the same time, the Gender and Development Focal Person of the CHED Central Office.

**[03:31:11.820] CHED Regional Director IV Edna Imelda F. Legazpi** (asr-003747)  
To answer po, Representative Elago's questions,

**[03:31:18.920] CHED Regional Director IV Edna Imelda F. Legazpi** (asr-003748)  
Number one po, we have ongoing survey about the compliance of the different HEIs in relation to our CMO.

**[03:31:27.860] CHED Regional Director IV Edna Imelda F. Legazpi** (asr-003749)  
Number one, series of 2015, ito po yung Safe Spaces Act or yung Bawal Bastos Law.

**[03:31:34.660] CHED Regional Director IV Edna Imelda F. Legazpi** (asr-003750)  
At the moment, Madam Chair,

**[03:31:37.040] CHED Regional Director IV Edna Imelda F. Legazpi** (asr-003751)  
we have around, out of our 2,347 HEIs, and out of that, 561 po yung SUCs, including the campuses or external campuses,

**[03:31:51.400] CHED Regional Director IV Edna Imelda F. Legazpi** (asr-003752)  
we have monitored through our regional offices, the compliance to the Bawal Bastos Law or Safe Spaces Act Law.

**[03:32:02.240] CHED Regional Director IV Edna Imelda F. Legazpi** (asr-003753)  
Ito po'y embedded sa

**[03:32:04.920] CHED Regional Director IV Edna Imelda F. Legazpi** (asr-003754)  
kanilang student SAS monitoring and evaluation.

**[03:32:11.340] CHED Regional Director IV Edna Imelda F. Legazpi** (asr-003755)  
Kasi po dahil wala po talagang budget for specific monitoring and evaluation of GAD-related projects,

**[03:32:20.540] CHED Regional Director IV Edna Imelda F. Legazpi** (asr-003756)  
what we do is we incorporate the monitoring and evaluation in the programs po of our student affairs and services.

**[03:32:32.240] CHED Regional Director IV Edna Imelda F. Legazpi** (asr-003757)  
So Madam Chair and Congressman Elago,

**[03:32:36.860] CHED Regional Director IV Edna Imelda F. Legazpi** (asr-003758)  
we would just give our report as to the exact number of amount or budget that was given to the different regional offices

**[03:32:51.400] CHED Regional Director IV Edna Imelda F. Legazpi** (asr-003759)  
in relation to the monitoring and evaluation of our compliance to Safe Spaces Act.

**[03:32:59.860] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003760)  
Madam Chair, hindi lang po sa Safe Spaces Act.

**[03:33:02.720] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003762)  
Kundi dun sa 1995 natin na Anti-Sexual Harassment Act.

**[03:33:07.160] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003763)  
Nabanggit niyo ho yung tungkol sa mga public universities and colleges natin.

**[03:33:11.320] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003764)  
Pero paano naman sa mga pribadong pamantasan at kolehyo,

**[03:33:14.900] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003765)  
natitiyak po ba natin na meron silang mga functional na Committee on Decorum and Investigation

**[03:33:20.400] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003766)  
para sa panahon na meron pong magsusumbong, may mag-uulat, may kaso na ihahain, ay merong tutugon sa kanila at tutulong sa antas

**[03:33:30.580] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003767)  
ng kanilang paaralan.

**[03:33:33.600] CHED Regional Director IV Edna Imelda F. Legazpi** (asr-003768)  
Madam Chair, based po doon sa IRR ng aming CMO1 Series of 2015,

**[03:33:41.220] CHED Regional Director IV Edna Imelda F. Legazpi** (asr-003769)  
nakapaloob po doon na ang compliance sa batas through establishments of CODIS ay talaga pong tinatry po ng Commission to really get the figures.

**[03:33:55.600] CHED Regional Director IV Edna Imelda F. Legazpi** (asr-003770)  
So we will submit po our data.

**[03:34:00.040] CHED Regional Director IV Edna Imelda F. Legazpi** (asr-003771)  
Because at the moment we are asking for all HEIs, whether private or private, their actual compliance.

**[03:34:08.480] CHED Regional Director IV Edna Imelda F. Legazpi** (asr-003772)  
But let me just quote our recent survey that at the moment we have 66% na functional po yung CODI.

**[03:34:20.200] CHED Regional Director IV Edna Imelda F. Legazpi** (asr-003773)

**[03:36:15.840] Legal Education Board Commissioner Justine Kyle D. Mapalo** (asr-003790)

**[03:36:21.320] Legal Education Board Commissioner Justine Kyle D. Mapalo** (asr-003791)  
So the first one is the Clinical Legal Education Program or the CLEP.

**[03:36:25.620] Legal Education Board Commissioner Justine Kyle D. Mapalo** (asr-003792)  
So it's free legal aid for the marginalized.

**[03:36:28.440] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003793)

**[03:36:32.860] Legal Education Board Commissioner Justine Kyle D. Mapalo** (asr-003795)  
Yes, ma'am.

**[03:36:33.960] Legal Education Board Commissioner Justine Kyle D. Mapalo** (asr-003796)  
So primarily, yung Clinical Legal Education Program or the CLEP, which offers free legal aid for the marginalized.

**[03:36:40.520] Legal Education Board Commissioner Justine Kyle D. Mapalo** (asr-003797)  
So it's a referral system where law clinics grants and community access to justice.

**[03:36:45.480] Legal Education Board Commissioner Justine Kyle D. Mapalo** (asr-003798)

**[03:36:53.600] Legal Education Board Commissioner Justine Kyle D. Mapalo** (asr-003799)

**[03:37:00.540] Legal Education Board Commissioner Justine Kyle D. Mapalo** (asr-003800)

**[03:37:04.500] Legal Education Board Commissioner Justine Kyle D. Mapalo** (asr-003801)  
which focuses primarily on faculty development, quality assurance, research, and institutional modernization,

**[03:37:10.620] Legal Education Board Commissioner Justine Kyle D. Mapalo** (asr-003802)  
which as well got zero pesos in the NEP.

**[03:37:15.160] Legal Education Board Commissioner Justine Kyle D. Mapalo** (asr-003803)

**[03:37:19.040] Legal Education Board Commissioner Justine Kyle D. Mapalo** (asr-003804)  
which pertains to student scholarships, graduate faculty, development research, and international fellowships,

**[03:37:25.620] Legal Education Board Commissioner Justine Kyle D. Mapalo** (asr-003805)

**[03:37:29.700] Legal Education Board Commissioner Justine Kyle D. Mapalo** (asr-003806)

**[03:46:02.910] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003900)  
Ang CHED ay isa rin ito sa aming priority program.

**[03:46:08.630] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003901)  
But as I have said, before we can come up with or strengthen an existing policy and come up with a new program, we really know what is happening in the ground.

**[03:46:21.250] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003902)  
So we really conducted a CHED student survey safety analytical portal.

**[03:46:28.470] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003903)  
So any student across these 5.8 million students are free to visit our website at ilalagay nila yung mga complaints nila.

**[03:46:42.990] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003904)  
And one of the five top incidents na nakuha namin is bullying, intimidation, and hazing, person-directed violence and threat, digital and online threats, property-related incidents, transport and community safety, dormitory and boarding house safety.

**[03:47:07.350] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003905)  
Ito po yung data namin ngayon, but we also identified the best practices and those concerns that are not addressed.

**[03:47:20.410] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003906)  
Yun po yung basis namin sa pag-come up ng pag-strengthen ng existing policy or new policy po.

**[03:47:28.630] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003907)  
Madami po kaming na-identify ding good practices nila, tulad ng security.

**[03:47:35.230] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003908)  
There is a security personnel assigned now sa mga buildings.

**[03:47:39.910] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003909)  
Functional guard post, controlled entry-exit, nako-control na po.

**[03:47:45.170] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003910)  
The ID and visitor registration are strictly implemented.

**[03:47:49.950] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003911)  
Adequate surrounding lighting, ini-encourage namin.

**[03:47:53.310] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003912)  
In fact, we have a proposal to have a net zero campus

**[03:47:57.110] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003913)  
para po hindi all through the night may light.

**[03:48:01.250] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003914)  
Emergency alarm public address system.

**[03:48:05.450] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003915)  
24-hour security deployment.

**[03:48:07.470] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003916)  
Functional CCTV.

**[03:48:09.070] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003917)  
Itong functional CCTV po, lahat ng mga SUCs are required to have this.

**[03:48:14.970] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003918)  
But part of their budget na inilalagay nila ngayon,

**[03:48:19.250] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003919)  
and then vehicle gate control.

**[03:48:21.110] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003920)  
So lahat po ito mino-monitor namin,

**[03:48:23.590] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003921)  
yung mga possible windows where we can

**[03:48:27.990] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003922)  
minimize external intrusion to the

**[03:48:30.650] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003923)  
campus. On top of this, we are revisiting

**[03:48:34.030] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003924)  
the safety spaces guidelines.

**[03:51:05.620] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003954)  
2025 to 2027.

**[03:51:07.380] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003955)  
Madam Chair.

**[03:51:08.260] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003956)  
Yeah, thank you very much for that question and concern, Kong.

**[03:51:13.700] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003957)

**[03:51:20.760] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003958)

**[03:51:25.920] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003959)

**[03:51:28.980] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003960)

**[03:51:34.160] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003961)  
Because before we finance,

**[03:51:35.620] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003962)

**[03:52:19.350] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003970)

**[03:52:19.700] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003971)  
po.

**[03:52:21.400] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003972)  
For the formula of the release of the free higher education,

**[03:52:27.340] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003973)  
deficiency of free higher education in the past four years,

**[03:52:31.140] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003974)

**[03:52:35.620] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003975)  
Between CHED and DBM.

**[03:52:48.980] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003979)

**[03:52:51.900] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003980)

**[03:52:54.540] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003981)  
So, as to the calculation, we came to an agreement

**[03:52:58.080] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003982)

**[03:53:02.620] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003983)

**[03:53:04.980] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003984)

**[03:53:08.720] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003985)

**[03:53:13.640] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003986)

**[03:53:15.420] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003987)

**[03:53:19.160] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003988)

**[03:53:21.880] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003989)

**[03:53:24.740] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003990)

**[03:53:29.860] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003991)  
Okay.

si Kong Sandro.
