**[04:16:08.500] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-004608)  
Thank you, the Honorable Angara.

**[04:16:10.400] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-004609)  
And now, we recognize also from the majority, the Honorable Brian Poe.

**[04:16:16.040] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004610)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[04:16:17.140] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-004611)  
Thank you, po.

**[04:16:20.480] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004612)  
Maraming salamat po, Madam Chair.

**[04:16:22.240] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004613)  
First and foremost, I would like to welcome the new head of CHED,

**[04:16:26.780] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004614)  
I hope the new position is suiting you well.

**[04:16:31.000] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004615)  
But I also want to note that CHED has had many improvements,

**[04:16:35.000] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004616)  
especially in the downloading of scholarships.

**[04:16:38.500] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004617)  
I believe the partnership with Land Bank is making a huge difference.

**[04:16:43.480] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004618)  
Could you share a little bit about that?

**[04:16:45.020] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004619)  
Kasi I want to inspire confidence in this committee,

**[04:16:49.080] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004620)  
that if we give you more budget for scholarships,

**[04:16:52.040] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004621)  
you are able to efficiently disperse that among the students.

**[04:16:55.600] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004622)  
Tell us more about that program, po.

**[04:16:57.680] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004623)  
Yes, po.

**[04:16:59.340] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004624)  
The CHED Land Bank program is dedicated to expedite promptly

**[04:17:06.060] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004625)  
the distribution of scholarship grants.

**[04:17:10.040] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004626)  
This emanated from a problem na nagtatagal ng

**[04:17:15.160] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004627)  
one sem, two sem or even worse,

**[04:17:17.820] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004628)  
magra-graduate na po because of the many intervening policy in between

**[04:17:22.920] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004629)  
ng release.

**[04:17:24.100] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004630)  
Like the...

**[04:17:24.820] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004631)  
The unliquidated policy, na kung may unliquidated program ang SUC,

**[04:17:32.040] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004632)  
hindi na madadownloadan in any forms kahit scholarship.

**[04:17:35.760] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004633)  
So, ang naisipan po namin na makipag-partner sa Land Bank.

**[04:17:42.000] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004634)  
Again, ang Land Bank naman is open with or without the CHED program with Land Bank.

**[04:17:48.100] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004635)  
Pwede naman mag-open yung mga bata.

**[04:17:50.300] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004636)  
But kung sila yung mag-open outside this program,

**[04:17:54.900] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004637)  
ay meron silang maintaining balance na mahirap i-maintain ng estudyante.

**[04:17:59.800] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004638)  
So, under this CHED Land Bank program,

**[04:18:02.700] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004639)  
ay walang, walang, walang maintaining balance

**[04:18:06.400] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004640)  
at doon na ididiretso from the after-validation regional office validation,

**[04:18:12.460] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004641)  
itaas sa central office yung kanilang application,

**[04:18:18.300] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004642)  
and then maida-download na po.

**[04:18:23.880] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-004643)  
Madam Chair, can I add?

**[04:18:25.500] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-004644)  
With the indulgence of the congressman.

**[04:18:27.260] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-004645)  
Yes.

**[04:18:27.660] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-004646)  
The Honorable SDS Hernandez.

**[04:18:30.360] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-004647)  
Actually, I think three, four years ago,

**[04:18:34.600] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-004648)  
isa ako sa nag-i-insist na dapat yung mga subsidy

**[04:18:40.860] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-004649)  
should be received by the student directly na dapat i-ATM.

**[04:18:46.680] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-004650)  
Kasi karamihan sa kanila,

**[04:18:48.540] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-004651)  
pag binaba mo sa eskwelahan,

**[04:18:51.720] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-004652)  
lahat ng deduction nandun na.

**[04:18:54.920] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-004653)  
Minsan yung deduction hindi makatarungan,

**[04:18:57.940] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-004654)  
hindi rin sila makakareklamo,

**[04:18:59.700] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-004655)  
kasi the threat na itanggal sila sa TES,

**[04:19:04.340] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-004656)  
kasi hindi nila masyadong naiintindihan eh.

**[04:19:06.580] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-004657)  
Akala nila it was the school giving the TES,

**[04:19:09.360] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-004658)  
dahil kontrolado ng school yung pondo.

**[04:19:12.200] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-004659)  
By the time they received their money,

**[04:19:13.880] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-004660)  
all the deduction,

**[04:19:15.560] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-004661)  
diba?

**[04:19:16.820] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-004662)  
Minsan solo, na isang eskwelahan,

**[04:19:19.340] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-004663)  
lahat kanila yun,

**[04:19:20.800] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-004664)  
pero sa kanila na pupunta.

**[04:19:22.720] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-004665)  
So it's so unfair to the students,

**[04:19:25.780] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-004666)  
dahil ayaw nilang magreklamo,

**[04:19:28.260] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-004667)  
dahil baka matanggal sila.

**[04:19:30.910] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-004668)  
So I'm glad that finally na nagagawa na ito and of course giving justice to the deserving students na sila ang magdideside kung ano ang deduction na ibabawas sa kanila nung eskwelahan dahil sila na mismo ang humahawak ng pondo.

**[04:19:51.380] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-004669)  
Again, maraming salamat Ched for introducing this reform.

**[04:19:55.520] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-004670)  
Thank you.

**[04:19:56.140] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004671)  
Thank you po.

**[04:19:58.220] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004672)  
About 80% na ang na-distributan po.

**[04:20:02.320] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004673)  
About 700,000 plus.

**[04:20:05.140] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004674)  
Ayan, exact.

**[04:20:06.400] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004675)  
Real time po kasi ito.

**[04:20:07.900] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004676)  
We have a real time digital system with land bank.

**[04:20:11.300] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004677)  
So as of this time,

**[04:20:12.740] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004678)  
3.30, meron ng 722,751 students.

**[04:20:20.300] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-004679)  
And congratulations, Chair, kasi kalmado ka na ngayon.

**[04:20:28.180] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-004681)  
All right.

**[04:20:29.440] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-004682)  
Yes, the Honorable, please continue.

**[04:20:31.560] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004683)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[04:20:33.180] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004684)  
I wanted to highlight that because you said you addressed 80% of the backlog of our scholars and we have how many again, 700 something thousand?

**[04:20:44.520] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004685)  
722,000.

**[04:20:45.880] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004685-split-1)  
out of 900 plus thousand.

**[04:20:47.880] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004686)  
So that's 722,000 students who now have bank accounts with zero maintaining balance.

**[04:20:54.400] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004687)  
Yes po.

**[04:20:54.840] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004688)  
Madam Chair, this addresses two problems immediately.

**[04:20:57.740] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004689)  
One is the efficiency of government scholarships and the second is financial literacy.

**[04:21:03.920] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004690)  
You are immediately teaching students how to open a bank account, how to save money and how to maintain a bank account.

**[04:21:11.440] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004691)  
We are banking the unbanked.

**[04:21:13.300] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004692)  
We're creating another generation of students who now know how to save money.

**[04:21:18.680] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004693)  
Madam Chair, this is a very important step forward for Ched and I believe we should continue to support it.

**[04:21:25.140] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004694)  
I also saw that in your budget, you have a 214% increase, I believe, or sorry, a 150% increase in the budget for the Bagong Pilipinas Merit Scholarship.

**[04:21:38.360] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004695)  
Is this correct?

**[04:21:41.740] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004696)  
Yes po.

**[04:21:43.540] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004697)  
Can you tell me a little bit about this program?

**[04:21:46.240] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004698)  
I believe, obviously, it's for our merit scholars, our top performing students.

**[04:21:50.480] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004699)  
How many students are supported by this program?

**[04:21:53.680] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004700)  
Yeah.

**[04:21:54.280] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004701)  
Thank you very much.

**[04:21:55.220] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004702)  
Foremost, I would like to credit His Excellency, our President, kasi tinanong niya noon.

**[04:22:00.800] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004703)  
Kasi I really am very thankful.

**[04:22:03.220] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004704)  
Sabi niya, bakit ang dami nating, ang EDCOM release na madaming programs na hindi tine-take ng mga estudyante.

**[04:22:15.460] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004705)  
Bakit ang daming attrition?

**[04:22:17.380] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004706)  
That was the question.

**[04:22:18.680] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004707)  
So we came up with the proposal.

**[04:22:20.380] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004708)  
The proposal caters the top five

**[04:22:23.480] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004709)  
Graduates of senior high school who are willing first to enroll to the ten priority programs identified by EDCOM.

**[04:22:33.140] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004710)  
Number two, they are accepted to a top performing university.

**[04:22:38.880] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004711)  
May criteria po eh yung university.

**[04:22:41.160] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004712)  
Whether state university or private.

**[04:22:44.560] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004713)  
And the student will receive kung private siya, subsidized.

**[04:22:50.400] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004714)  
Mga 80,000 ba?

**[04:22:52.000] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004715)  
Can you give me the number?

**[04:22:53.400] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004716)  
Kung private siya, 90,000.

**[04:22:56.760] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004717)  
Kung sa government naman, state university, libre kasi ang tuition fee.

**[04:23:02.240] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004718)  
In terms of allowance, ang isang estudyante nakaka-receive ng 45,000 per semester.

**[04:23:11.400] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004719)  
For academic year.

**[04:23:17.300] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004720)  
Yeah.

**[04:23:17.880] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004721)  
Yun po yung ano.

**[04:23:19.040] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004722)  
Pero ngayon po, of the 20,000 slots for 2026,

**[04:23:25.020] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004723)  
sa dami ng magagaling na nag-graduate,

**[04:23:28.000] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004724)  
we receive already more than 39,000 applicants.

**[04:23:33.620] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004725)  
But how do we decide, how do we distribute that, Madam Chair, if I may ask?

**[04:23:39.380] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004726)  
We have a formula developed.

**[04:23:42.340] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004727)  
We have a formula developed.

**[04:23:45.320] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004728)  
So wala pong bahid ng irregularities doon.

**[04:23:51.040] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004729)  
Kasi may, based on the grade, based on the per capita income of the students,

**[04:23:57.000] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004730)  
meron ding point yung siya ay first generation in the family.

**[04:24:03.040] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004731)  
Ano pa, Cor?

**[04:24:05.580] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004732)  
And then the...

**[04:24:14.954] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004732-split-1)  
Yup.

**[04:24:16.580] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004732-split-2)  
Yup.

**[04:24:17.520] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004733)  
Yup.

**[04:24:19.960] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004734)  
Yup.

**[04:24:21.480] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004735)  
Proportionately distributed based on the number of graduates of senior high school per region.

**[04:24:28.460] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004736)  
Na nasa top five.

**[04:24:30.760] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004737)  
May formula po doon.

**[04:24:32.980] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004738)  
So it's truly, it's really merit.

**[04:24:35.640] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004740)  
So there's no aspect of economics behind it.

**[04:24:38.320] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004741)  
Yes po.

**[04:24:38.800] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004742)  
Okay.

**[04:24:39.440] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004743)  
So it doesn't matter if you're considered poorest of the poor.

**[04:24:42.420] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004744)  
You don't necessarily have a leg up over someone else.

**[04:24:46.580] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004745)  
It's really based on grades.

**[04:24:48.120] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004746)  
Zero to two million at least na household income.

**[04:24:52.440] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004747)  
Irregular family naman hindi naman.

**[04:24:54.340] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004748)  
Ah, okay.

**[04:24:54.980] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004749)  
So it's only those with zero to, to two million pesos or one million pesos annual income?

**[04:25:01.000] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004750)  
Hindi naman only po.

**[04:25:01.600] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004751)  
But we give priority sa zero to two million income plus grades.

**[04:25:08.540] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004752)  
And then do you know the distribution so far?

**[04:25:11.700] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004753)  
Where are most of your scholars from currently?

**[04:25:14.180] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004754)  
Yes po.

**[04:25:14.520] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004755)  
Meron kami.

**[04:25:15.320] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004756)  
Because it's easy now to locate because there is an equation and they are generated digitally.

**[04:25:23.260] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004757)  
They distribute.

**[04:25:24.600] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004758)  
So, halimbawa, in Region 1, they have a top performer.

**[04:25:27.060] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004759)  
They have a graduate, top performing graduate of 10,000.

**[04:25:31.960] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004760)  
Region two, they have 5,000.

**[04:25:34.000] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004761)  
May kanya-kanyang equity.

**[04:25:35.740] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004762)  
May ratio po.

**[04:25:37.660] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004763)  
We will provide you copy, Kong.

**[04:25:39.600] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004764)  
Sige po.

**[04:25:40.280] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004765)  
So moving quickly, I noticed that even with all of this, there is a significant decrease in your budget.

**[04:25:48.260] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004766)  
What specific items were hit?

**[04:25:51.900] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004767)  
Is there anything in particular that you feel is a critical mistake on the part of the DBM to remove from the budget?

**[04:26:01.460] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004768)  
We proposed for 70.2 billion kong.

**[04:26:05.580] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004769)  
And the data, the basis was never a magic based on data.

**[04:26:11.580] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004770)  
We got 35 billion for NEP.

**[04:26:14.460] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004771)  
The gap is 34 billion.

**[04:26:16.940] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004772)  
In my presentation na kanina, we identified the big ticket na maasasagasaan.

**[04:26:23.000] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004773)  
UAQTEA, 29 billion ang kulang.

**[04:26:26.500] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004774)  
Scholarship, 697 million.

**[04:26:29.800] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004775)  
Yung digital transformation natin namin, sana po ibigay.

**[04:26:34.380] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004776)  
Masayang yung aming digital system na na-develop.

**[04:26:37.740] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004777)  
331.

**[04:26:39.800] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004778)  
258 million sa monitoring and evaluation function.

**[04:26:43.700] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004779)  
And the others include many things.

**[04:26:46.780] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004780)  
So ang total po is 34.8 billion ang kulang.

**[04:26:50.860] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004781)  
Madam Chair, did I hear that right?

**[04:26:54.060] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004782)  
97 million was lost from scholarships?

**[04:27:00.120] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004783)  
697 million po.

**[04:27:03.060] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004784)  
697 million removed from scholarships?

**[04:27:05.800] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004785)  
Yes po.

**[04:27:07.240] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004786)  
Ilan ang beneficiary?

**[04:27:11.080] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004787)  
Yes po.

**[04:27:12.460] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004788)  
And then we can submit to you the breakdown of this.

**[04:27:16.840] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004789)  
Okay.

**[04:27:17.600] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004790)  
Just wanted to highlight that, Madam Chair, so that maybe we can look into these scholarships that were removed.

**[04:27:23.080] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004791)  
And ask the question as to why these scholarships were removed when the students are very deserving.

**[04:27:29.160] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004792)  
Again, moving along quickly, we want to make sure that our education is competitive.

**[04:27:35.160] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004793)  
And that it will remain competitive, especially in the age of artificial intelligence.

**[04:27:41.040] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004794)  
I believe that you have created an AI roadmap.

**[04:27:45.860] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004795)  
Where are we in that roadmap currently?

**[04:27:48.880] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004796)  
And what, what are we funding in the 2027 GAA to support our AI programs and initiatives?

**[04:27:56.700] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004797)  
Nasaan yung listahan?

**[04:27:58.100] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004798)  
Yes, sir.

**[04:28:00.840] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004799)  
We are aware of that.

**[04:28:02.920] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004800)  
The, what's that?

**[04:28:06.640] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004801)  
Adoption of AI technology comes faster than the policy.

**[04:28:11.340] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004802)  
And comes faster than the capacity building among SUCs.

**[04:28:17.300] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004803)  
So to control that at least, we came up already with the National AI in Higher Education Policy.

**[04:28:25.260] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004804)  
Meron na po kami.

**[04:28:26.280] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004805)  
And now we are now cascading to state university and colleges, including HEI.

**[04:28:31.180] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004806)  
That they must come up with an AI policy to avoid, what's that?

**[04:28:38.700] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004807)  
Denuding the human thinking capacity of our students.

**[04:28:44.100] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004808)  
And in order to, and we focus on this priority to really capacitate our teachers, faculty.

**[04:28:52.560] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004809)  
So we funded already.

**[04:28:54.280] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004810)  
Several programs cascaded it to different state university po.

**[04:29:02.300] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004811)  
And total is, hindi lang five million.

**[04:29:08.160] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004812)  
Yung total na in-locate namin, may AI in health sciences application.

**[04:29:16.180] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004813)  
May AI in financial literacy.

**[04:29:18.980] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004814)  
May AI in fishing.

**[04:29:21.020] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004815)  
May AI in agriculture.

**[04:29:23.340] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004816)  
So while we are crafting the massive manpower development for AI.

**[04:29:28.900] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004817)  
Madam Chair, I want to highlight the fact that CHED is taking the initiative on this.

**[04:29:32.800] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004818)  
And that their framework is actually pretty much done already.

**[04:29:35.420] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004819)  
And being cascaded.

**[04:29:37.100] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004820)  
Tayo. Hindi pa pumapasang AI framework natin dito sa House.

**[04:29:42.060] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004821)  
That bill is still not yet with the Senate.

**[04:29:45.220] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004822)  
And I hope we can catch up to CHED.

**[04:29:47.220] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004823)  
But that brings up an important note.

**[04:29:50.900] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004824)  
May I ask?

**[04:29:52.060] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004825)  
Because I've been going through your budget.

**[04:29:53.360] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004826)  
And I haven't noticed.

**[04:29:54.460] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004827)  
There seems to be no budget for research and development.

**[04:29:58.280] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004828)  
No.

**[04:29:58.860] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004829)  
And.

**[04:29:59.500] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004830)  
I want the committee to take note that CHED's mission is not just to have students graduate.

**[04:30:06.060] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004831)  
It's also capacity building.

**[04:30:08.040] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004832)  
It's also innovation.

**[04:30:09.280] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004833)  
It's also furthering research and development because that is also the job of the academe.

**[04:30:14.900] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004834)  
You look at universities around the world, and they contribute to the development of their countries through research and development in higher education.

**[04:30:24.720] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004835)  
So I want to know if there's a budget here for that.

**[04:30:30.000] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004836)  
We have.

**[04:30:31.060] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004837)  
We focus.

**[04:30:31.780] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004838)  
This is one of our priority area under our Achieve agenda.

**[04:30:36.100] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004839)  
It is represented in letter C, centralized, harmonized SDG base, R&D agenda.

**[04:30:46.160] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004840)  
We focus on the 17 SDG, and we already have several initiatives here.

**[04:30:53.300] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004841)  
We will submit it to you.

**[04:30:55.440] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004842)  
But as to the funding, as to the funding,

**[04:30:58.860] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004843)  
the funding,

**[04:31:00.040] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004844)  
that we included in our proposal is the capacity building of faculty researchers.

**[04:31:05.940] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004845)  
But as to the project itself, we lodge it to HEDF.

**[04:31:11.560] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004846)  
So marami na po kami mga indicative application on the research.

**[04:31:17.940] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004847)  
So nandun po sa HEDF.

**[04:31:19.740] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004848)  
In fact, if time may allow me, we have, yeah, we allotted 1.4 billion from the HEDF pillars funded project po.

**[04:31:33.260] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004849)  
It's 15% of the total program that we allotted from HEDF.

**[04:31:40.440] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004850)  
Thank you.

**[04:31:41.400] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004851)  
I bring that up because our chair here came from research.

**[04:31:46.640] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004852)  
And in fact, my first job in government was as a researcher.

**[04:31:50.800] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004853)  
So I do believe that funding research and developing it is very important for the development of our entire nation.

**[04:31:57.660] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004854)  
And keeping us competitive in an age of artificial intelligence.

**[04:32:01.300] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004855)  
And innovation.

**[04:32:02.980] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004856)  
So hopefully, we can continue to fund those programs.

**[04:32:06.900] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004857)  
Madam Chair, if I may go back real quick to the question earlier about the distribution of scholars.

**[04:32:14.040] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004858)

that at the click of a finger, we can, we can follow through kung nasaan ang majority ng scholars across the...

If this scholarship program had an increase in budget,

**[04:34:04.680] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004885)

**[04:34:08.040] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004886)

**[04:34:14.180] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004888)

**[04:34:23.780] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004889)

**[04:34:32.360] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004890)

**[04:34:42.480] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-004891)  
So 870 ang ongoing ngayon.

**[05:17:50.540] Rep. Brian Raymund S. Yamsuan** (asr-005574)

**[05:18:12.500] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-005577)

**[05:18:32.200] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-005580)

**[05:18:40.640] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-005581)

**[05:18:43.800] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-005582)

**[05:18:48.760] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-005583)

**[05:18:58.400] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-005586)

**[05:19:03.780] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-005588)  
Simple lang po.

**[05:19:05.100] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-005589)  
So one of the major mandate of CHED is to...

**[05:19:09.860] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-005590)  
to set a standard and also regulatory function.

**[05:19:14.460] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-005591)

**[05:19:21.140] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-005592)  
So meron po yun.

**[05:19:22.540] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-005593)  
And this is written by our technical panel.

**[05:19:27.780] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-005594)  
And we also have what we call the Certificate of Program Compliance.

**[05:19:32.600] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-005595)  
Ito po ang mahirap na kailangan i-comply before anyone can offer a degree program.

**[05:19:39.460] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-005596)  
Inside the Certificate of Program Compliance,

**[05:19:42.980] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-005597)  
may mga kwalifikasyon ng faculty

**[05:19:45.360] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-005598)

**[05:19:48.260] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-005599)  
in terms of number.

**[05:19:50.380] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-005600)  
May compliance when it comes to facility.

**[05:19:54.660] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-005601)  
May compliance when it comes to sustainability.

**[05:19:57.700] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-005602)  
What else?

**[05:20:04.120] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-005603)  
Yeah, institutional development platform.

**[05:20:09.930] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-005604)  
So kung yun po ay mamit,

**[05:20:12.420] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-005605)  
and then if you are referring...

**[05:20:14.840] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-005606)

**[05:20:17.320] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-005607)

**[05:20:25.760] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-005608)

**[05:20:30.720] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-005609)

**[05:20:39.820] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-005610)

**[05:20:43.240] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-005611)  
So ganun po.

**[05:20:44.500] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-005612)  
Simple yun.

**[05:30:33.800] Rep. Jude A. Acidre** (asr-005829-split-1)  
Thank you, Madam Chair. Last question na po ito at alam ko yung medyo ilang oras na hong sumasagot ang ating chairperson ng CHED. Susundan ko lang ho yung tanong kanina ni Kong Brian para lang mabigyan yung mga kababayan natin

**[05:30:51.380] Rep. Jude A. Acidre** (asr-005830)  
at of course yung mga kasamahan natin ng better picture ng kalagayan po ng ating mga LUCs. Tama po ba Madam Chair na hindi po lahat ng LUCs sa ngayon

**[05:31:01.200] Rep. Jude A. Acidre** (asr-005831)  
qualified or accredited or paano po ba ang tamang term na covered sila ng UACTEA ng free higher education?

**[05:31:11.020] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-005832)  
Tama po.

**[05:31:13.380] Rep. Jude A. Acidre** (asr-005833)  
Tama po. So, ilan po ang covered, ilan po ang hindi covered?

**[05:31:18.260] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-005834)  
Yes po. To date, there are 170 local universities and colleges established through their local

**[05:31:27.780] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-005835)  
governance. Only 116

**[05:31:31.420] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-005836)  
with institutional recognition. Yung wala, yung walang IR is 54.

**[05:31:40.460] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-005837)  
Institutional recognition is based on their compliance on the criteria sa, we set, like COPC and among others.

**[05:31:49.340] Rep. Jude A. Acidre** (asr-005838)  
So ibig sabihin, Madam Chair, itong mga may institutional recognition lang po ang masasabi nating covered ng free higher education.

**[05:31:58.140] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-005839)  
Unfortunately, with a heavy heart, yes po.

**[05:32:02.440] Rep. Jude A. Acidre** (asr-005840)  
At kung atin po silang tutulungan na maka-comply sa requirements ng institutional recognition, sila po ay mapagbibigyan sa free higher education.

**[05:32:14.620] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-005841)  
Yes po, that's the developmental part of our Commission on Higher Education. Pero, yes po.

**[05:32:23.280] Rep. Jude A. Acidre** (asr-005842)  
Tinanong ko po yun, Madam Chair, paalala lang po sa mga kasamahan namin

**[05:32:26.700] Rep. Jude A. Acidre** (asr-005843)

**[05:32:40.040] Rep. Jude A. Acidre** (asr-005844)

**[05:32:48.200] Rep. Jude A. Acidre** (asr-005845)

**[05:32:58.080] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-005846)  
Thank you for asking that, Kong Jude.

**[05:33:00.520] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-005847)  
Now, in fact, this is one of the major challenges in higher education.

**[05:33:04.740] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-005848)  
Because the offering, first is the offering of the local universities and colleges.

**[05:33:10.640] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-005849)  
They go beyond what is mandated by law.

**[05:33:14.220] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-005850)  
Sometimes they overlap with the offerings of recognized state universities and colleges within the region.

**[05:33:21.520] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-005851)  
To the extent of offering masteral and PhD degree program.

**[05:33:26.340] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-005852)  
So ngayon sa side ng CHED, gumawa na po kami ng guidelines for consultation

**[05:33:33.520] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-005853)  
And with the oversight of Commissioner Mitz, we are now trying to instill policy reform para po makontrol at maiwasan ang mushrooming ng local universities and colleges.

**[05:33:55.000] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-005854)  
Because it gives

**[05:33:56.700] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-005855)  
many, many effect.

**[05:33:58.980] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-005856)  
In fact, mas madaling dumami ang local universities and colleges than state universities and colleges, Kong.

**[05:34:07.800] Rep. Jude A. Acidre** (asr-005857)

**[07:35:19.080] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005898)

**[07:35:34.780] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005900)

**[07:35:40.680] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005901)

**[07:35:50.060] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005902)  
and roughly 4.8 billion sourced from the GAA.

**[07:35:54.980] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005903)

**[07:36:01.340] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005904)  
because hinihintay din po ng ating mga state universities and colleges yun pong pambayad doon po sa kakulangan sa implementation po ng Higher Free Tertiary Education Law nitong mga nakaraang taon.

**[07:36:19.220] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-005906)  
Yeah, thank you very much, ma'am. Good evening, Chair, and congratulations.

**[07:36:24.660] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-005907)  
And thank you for waiting. Thank you to the baby.

**[07:36:28.900] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-005908)  
Yes, ma'am. We already signed a joint memorandum circular with DBM and CHED.

**[07:36:37.040] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-005909)  
So two weeks ago, if I am not mistaken, they released already 9.7 billion for the deficiency.

**[07:36:48.140] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-005910)

**[07:37:04.140] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-005911)  
May mga hindi pa nabigyan.

**[07:37:07.440] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-005912)

**[07:37:21.980] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005913)  
Thank you, Chair Shirley. Just wanted to clarify po because roughly 12 billion pesos po yun, diba?

**[07:37:28.020] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005914)  
I'm looking at the status that was given just now. 9.4 billion allocated for those with deficient billings from 2022 to 2024. Without submission is just 341 million.

**[07:37:49.080] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005915)  
Pwede ko po pang tanungin kasi po, that adds up to just roughly 9.7 billion.

**[07:37:58.340] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005916)  
12 billion. So nasan po yung remainder? The remainder of the 12 billion?

**[07:38:02.300] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005917)  
Nasa Bureau of Treasury.

**[07:38:04.220] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005918)  
So kanino po mapupunta tayo? Dito po sa first cluster na may deficiency, doon po sa walang submission? Kasi po if we add it all up, hindi po nag-add up eh.

**[07:38:18.260] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-005919)  
Itong 9.7 kasi, ito po, initially 12.7 billion ang na-compute natin.

**[07:38:26.320] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-005920)  
Because, can you help me here?

**[07:38:28.500] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-005921)  
Because of the faculty-student ratio. And then there was a little discrepancy of the, the submission by SUC.

**[07:38:39.140] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-005922)  
So sabi namin, we need to stick to the initial submission because if this deficiency had been paid on time, yun ang natuloy.

**[07:38:49.440] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-005923)  
So we did not entertain any more additional claims po.

**[07:38:57.340] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-005924)  
So, can Ma'am Alma?

**[07:39:01.420] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-005925)  
So, as of this date, 9.7 billion. No more claims. Additional?

**[07:39:14.100] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-005926)  
108.

**[07:39:15.660] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-005927)  
108 SUCs na po kasi ang nag-submit. That covers the 9.7 billion. And we are counting 113.

**[07:39:27.560] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005928)  
But, sir po kasi, Chair, even if you add the

**[07:39:32.280] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005929)  
The 97 account for the 9.4 billion, 97 SUCs, 11 SUCs account for the 341. So at 9.7 billion, that only accounts for 108 of the SUCs.

**[07:39:50.240] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005930)  
So how about the remainder? May nakikita din po ako dito na may 8. There are 8 without any deficiencies. So that only adds up to 116.

**[07:40:01.520] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005931)  
How about the other SUCs po?

**[07:40:09.540] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-005932)  
May mga chair? May mga SUC kasi na walang deficiency.

**[07:40:15.600] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-005933)  
Yes po.

**[07:40:16.300] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-005934)  
At may mga SUC na overpayment. Overpaid. So hindi po sila nakasali.

**[07:40:22.580] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-005935)  
So ito na lang. Kung ang hinahanap nyo po is hindi na maximize yung inalot natin na 7.8 billion. Totoo po yun.

**[07:40:33.960] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-005936)  
Kasi 9 points sa HEDF yun na iwan.

**[07:40:37.140] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-005937)  
I-exhaust namin lahat.

**[07:40:39.200] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-005938)  
Ang yata ang ginawa

**[07:40:40.360] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-005939)  
ni DBM is in-exhaust lahat yung 4 point something billion galing sa Congress, sa GAA. And then yung na iwan is yung sa HEDF.

**[07:40:52.660] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-005940)

**[08:19:09.620] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-006897)

**[08:19:11.880] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-006898)

**[08:19:24.640] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-006899)  
Kasi hindi

**[08:19:25.180] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-006900)  
naman niyo yun mapapagawa, ako yung magpapagawa

**[08:19:26.900] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-006901)

**[08:19:28.440] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-006902)

**[08:19:29.200] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-006903)

**[08:19:32.800] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-006905)

**[08:19:35.640] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-006906)  
Madam Chair, I move that the committee terminate its hearing on the proposed fiscal year 2027 budget of the Commission on Higher Education

**[08:19:47.440] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-006907)  
under the House Bill No. 10858, I so move.

**[08:19:51.660] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-006908)

**[08:19:54.560] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-006910)

**[08:19:57.560] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-006911)  
And duly seconded to terminate the hearing of the proposed 2027 budget of the CHED.

**[08:20:03.640] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-006912)  
Is there any objection?

**[08:20:05.640] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-006913)

**[08:20:07.220] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-006914)

**[08:20:23.580] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-006917)

**[08:20:47.440] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-006924)
